# Known Physical Bitcoin Attacks
A list of known attacks against Bitcoin / crypto asset owning entities that occurred in meatspace. NOTE: this list is not comprehensive; many attacks are not publicly reported. If you are aware of an attack that is not listed, please open an issue or pull request — see [CONTRIBUTING.md](./CONTRIBUTING.md) for what qualifies and how to add entries.

For interactive visual dashboards of this data, check out [our map](https://jlopp.github.io/physical-bitcoin-attacks/) and the [Crypto Crime Dashboard](https://cryptocrime-dashboard.vercel.app/)
For more comprehensive statistical breakdowns of attack attributes, check out [gart.io](https://gart.io/stats)
Another comprehensive list of attacks is available at [bitcoin-wrench-attacks.zaup.org](https://bitcoin-wrench-attacks.zaup.org/index.html)

For high level analyses and best practices, readers may be interested in this 2020 presentation: ["The Hodlguard- a primer on physical security in Bitcoin"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HUgPhPkS2yc) and [slides](https://prezi.com/view/P46hHguLiPvqA7aZ7gYR).
Also, Jameson's [2025 presentation](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bxjKAtxy6J4) with updated metrics and advice.
Finally, [this academic paper](./Wrench_Attacks_Research.pdf) analyzing physical attacks using Bitcointalk as a data source.

| 2020 | Unidentified | Wenzhou, Zhejiang, China | [1,000 BTC targeted (CNY 52.69 million according to the Rui'an public prosecutor's office) — nothing obtained — The defendants lured a bitcoin holder to a physical meeting under the pretext of teaching him bitcoin trading, then threatened him with a fake weapon to force him to hand over 1,000 BTC; when he refused, they threatened him with a folding knife, obtaining nothing. The court found attempted armed robbery and sentenced the principal offender to 4 years in prison and a fine of RMB 10,000, and the accomplice to 2 years and 6 months and RMB 6,000; the Rui'an public prosecutor's office had valued the 1,000 BTC at RMB 52.69 million at the time of the events. | January 14 2020 | Madho Singh | Charkhi Dadri, Haryana, India | [INR 4,500 in cash stolen (bitcoins targeted but not obtained) — On January 14, 2020, Madho Singh, from Guda village (Mahendragarh), was driving a friend's car toward Hisar when a group arriving in a Scorpio abducted him at gunpoint near Charkhi village, beat him, took 4,500 rupees from him, and then abandoned him near Satnali; the attackers, who had followed him for several days and had planted a GPS chip on his car, were seeking to seize his bitcoins. On March 2, 2020, the Dadri CIA arrested four suspects (Deependra, Arvind, Vinod, Sachin), who were placed in three days of police custody, with three accomplices still at large. The main accused, Deependra, was already facing prosecution for a similar 2017 case (Kanina police station) in which about 43 bitcoins, then worth more than 5 million rupees (50 lakh), had been transferred.](https://www.amarujala.com/haryana/charkhi-dadri/four-accused-of-kidnapping-young-man-arrested-for-grabbing-bitcoins-charkhidadri-news-rtk5451070156) |
| January 2020 | Mr. Zhang | Taichung, Taiwan | [TWD 2,780,000 (advertised purchase price for 100,000 USDT) — On January 14, 2020, Zhuang Menglun had advertised on Telegram the sale of 100,000 USDT to lure buyer Mr. Zhang, who came to the meeting with TWD 2.78 million in cash. When Zhang saw that the USDT had not been transferred, he tried to leave the vehicle: two minor accomplices (Lin, 19, and Wu, 17) held him back and stabbed him, one inflicting a 15 cm wound to his left chest, before pushing him, badly injured, onto the shoulder of the Taiwan Route 74 expressway and driving off with the money; he died at the hospital. The two minors were sentenced to 15 years in prison and, along with their parents, ordered to pay TWD 5.62 million in damages; the instigator Zhuang remains at large.](https://news.ltn.com.tw/news/society/breakingnews/3665737) [(source 2)](https://www.blocktempo.com/taiwan-usdt-otc-trad-scam-victum-stab-7-death/) |

| February 19 2020 | Liang Wen-yao | Taipei, Taiwan | [Unknown — cash from the handbag split among the perpetrators (proceeds of crime confiscated: TWD 12,000 and TWD 4,000); no cryptocurrency obtained, as the wallet had already been emptied — On the night of February 18-19, 2020, five men led by the victim's roommate — who knew he was profiting from cryptocurrency investments via a mobile app — rang the doorbell of his Taipei home at 12:58 a.m., with one of them showing his genuine coast guard service card while posing as a police officer on duty. They pinned him to the ground, took his phone and bag, brought him down in the elevator while again showing the fake police credential to the building's guard, then abducted him by car to a mountainous area of Linkou, where, with his hands tied behind his back, he was punched and kicked to force him to give up his passwords. As the cryptocurrency had already been transferred, the attackers split the cash from the bag and abandoned him around 3 a.m. near a landfill; the five were sentenced on October 15, 2020 to terms of 11 to 12 and a half years for gang kidnapping for ransom and aggravated robbery.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=SLDM%2C109%2C%E5%8E%9F%E8%A8%B4%2C3%2C20201015%2C3) |

| April 29 2020 | Marco Antônio Fagundes | Novo Hamburgo, Rio Grande do Sul, Brazil | [50 BTC demanded (BRL 2,814,899.18 according to police at the time); ransom never paid — On April 29, 2020, around 8:30 a.m. in Novo Hamburgo (Rio Grande do Sul), Marco Antônio Fagundes, one of the founding partners of InDeal — a Bitcoin-based pyramid scheme dismantled by the federal Operation Egypto in 2019 — was abducted by a group of former investors who felt wronged and demanded a ransom of 50 bitcoins from his family (BRL 2,814,899.18 at the time, about USD 445,000 to 520,000). The ransom was never paid, and the victim was released the same day around 5 p.m. in Canoas, after nine hours in captivity. The Rio Grande do Sul civil police, treating revenge as the motive, launched Operation 'Talião' on November 18, 2020: out of an estimated at least five participants, three men — an active-duty military police officer, a financial market trader, and the latter's father — were implicated and arrested, with warrants executed in Canoas, Sapucaia do Sul, Porto Alegre, and Campo Bom.](https://portaldobitcoin.uol.com.br/operacao-no-rs-investiga-trader-e-policial-por-sequestro-de-socio-de-piramide-financeira/) [(source 2)](https://livecoins.com.br/apos-sequestro-de-socio-da-indeal-pc-investiga-e-prende-tres/) |
| May 11 2020 | You Ke-hsun | Taichung, Taiwan | [TWD 3,848,000 in cash (agreed price of 130,000 USDT at TWD 29.8 per unit) — Burdened with gambling debts, Chen Sheng-lin led USDT buyer You Ke-hsun to believe via Telegram that he could sell him 130,000 USDT for TWD 3,848,000, and lured him on May 11, 2020 to the private room 麗禧廳 in the basement of the RICH19 club in Taichung, where the victim arrived with the cash. Once the money was counted and placed in a bag, Chen called in three accomplices stationed in an adjoining room: two grabbed the victim's arms while a third strangled him with both hands, allowing Chen to grab the bag and flee. You was injured (scratches to the hands and neck, a lumbar sprain, suspected fracture of a finger); the four men were sentenced on December 29, 2020 to 6 years 10 months and 6 years for gang armed robbery and to 1 year 6 months and 1 year 2 months for coercion, the latter sentence raised to 1 year 4 months on appeal on September 1, 2021.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TCDM%2C109%2C%E8%A8%B4%2C1662%2C20201229%2C1) [(source 2)](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TCHM%2C110%2C%E4%B8%8A%E8%A8%B4%2C502%2C20210901%2C1) |

| June 2020 | Mohammad _____ | Mashhad, Iran | ['Several billion tomans' worth of bitcoin (چند میلیارد تومان), exact amount not specified — Leaving a pyramid scheme meeting at a villa on Shahid Fakkouri Boulevard in Mashhad, 'Mohammad', 32, was surrounded near his Mercedes by three masked men armed with machetes, knives, and a firearm, who emerged from a stolen black Peugeot 405; an accomplice who had infiltrated the meeting had given the signal by text message. Taken to a garden villa rented two days earlier on Toos Boulevard, he was tortured and forced under threat of death to give up the password to the site where his bitcoin account was held, which the mastermind 'Saeed', 38, immediately used to transfer the assets; the victim was then abandoned, hands and feet bound, by the river on Shahnameh Boulevard 44. | September 2020 | Five foreigners | Castelo Branco, Portugal | [Tens of bitcoins, approximately EUR 3,000,000 at September 2020 value — In September 2020, a criminal group of nine people (eight men and one woman), all of foreign nationality, acting according to carefully planned "paramilitary-style" methods, invaded a rural property in the Castelo Branco area where five foreign citizens living in Portugal resided. The five occupants were held captive and one of them was forced, at gunpoint, to transfer tens of bitcoins (approximately EUR 3 million at the value of the time) to several wallets. The investigation by the Polícia Judiciária (Operation "Miragem", with Europol) led to the arrest of six men and one woman on December 12, 2023 in Portugal (searches in Lisbon, Loulé, Portimão, Monsanto, Penamacor) and two people in the United Kingdom; eight defendants were sentenced on March 25, 2026 by the Castelo Branco court to prison terms ranging from 3 years 11 months to 9 years.](https://www.policiajudiciaria.pt/roubo-bitcoins-operacao-miragem1/) [(source 2)](https://www.policiajudiciaria.pt/extraditado-do-reino-unido-para-portugal-por-roubo-agravado-e-sequestro/) |
| September 2020 | Unidentified couple | Anápolis, Goiás, Brazil | [555 BTC (valued at ~BRL 50,000,000 by G1 and Metrópoles in late 2022; or approximately USD 5.9 million at the September 2020 exchange rate, the date of the events) — In September 2020, in Anápolis, Goiás, two armed men overpowered the Brazilian wife of a German businessman as she returned from shopping, while a third accomplice entered the garage driving a vehicle; the couple was taken away, the home computer was seized, and both were driven to a vacant lot near the air base. Under threat ("if you don't type in the right codes, I'll kill you on the spot"), the victim transferred his 555 bitcoins, and the couple was abandoned on the spot four hours later. The investigation identified fifteen suspects split between an intellectual-authorship group in São Paulo and an operational group in Goiânia — including trader Edgar Acioli, the couple's first contact — and led to 32 search warrants on December 15, 2022 (BRL 1.2 million in cash, cars, and jewelry seized), but no arrests; traumatized, the couple left Brazil for Europe.](https://g1.globo.com/go/goias/noticia/2022/12/27/alemao-que-teve-r-50-milhoes-em-bitcoins-roubados-durante-sequestro-em-anapolis-voltou-para-europa-por-trauma-diz-advogado.ghtml) [(source 2)](https://www.metropoles.com/brasil/trauma-casal-sequestrado-por-r-50-mi-em-bitcoins-decide-deixar-pais) |
| September 18 2020 | 44-year-old woman | Ceará, Brazil | [BRL 1,000,000 demanded in bitcoin (≈ USD 187,000 at the September 2020 exchange rate) — ransom never paid — On Friday, September 18, 2020, in the afternoon, a 44-year-old woman was abducted in Ceará outside a dental clinic by two men who approached her as she got into her car, the scene being filmed by CCTV, with a rented Chevrolet Onix providing support. The mastermind, Calebe Barbosa do Carmo (25), who had promised BRL 10,000 to each of the kidnappers and was demanding BRL 1 million in bitcoin, claimed to have been wronged by the victim's sons in a cryptocurrency investment operation and had been planning the crime for a month. The civil police located the vehicle within twelve hours using the SPIA license-plate recognition system; Calebe was arrested in the act, confessed and revealed the hideout location, the victim was released without the ransom being paid, but the three kidnappers fled and remain wanted.](https://livecoins.com.br/sequestro-de-mulher-que-pedia-bitcoin-como-resgate-e-desarticulado-pela-policia/) |
| September 24 2020 | 46-year-old man | Paullo, Italy | [EUR 2,000 in cash + a laptop computer — On September 24, 2020, in Paullo, in the province of Milan, a 46-year-old Romanian man living in Verona, who had posted an online ad to buy and sell bitcoins, had arranged via WhatsApp with two fake sellers to hand over cash in exchange for cryptocurrency. Seeing the wad of banknotes, the two 17-year-old teenagers kicked and punched him in the face until he fell to the ground and tore away his backpack containing a laptop computer and EUR 2,000 in cash before fleeing. Identified thanks to CCTV footage and the victim's immediate complaint, the two minors were arrested on January 29, 2021, and a third 17-year-old minor, considered the "mastermind" of the operation, was arrested in late April 2021 by the Paullo carabinieri and placed in the Beccaria juvenile detention center in Milan.](https://www.leggo.it/italia/milano/milano_bitcoin_rapina_ultime_notizie-5927592.html) [(source 2)](https://www.affaritaliani.it/milano/offrono-bitcoin-poi-lo-rapinano-minorenni-arrestati-a-milano-719753.html) |

| October 13 2020 | Javier Moscuzza | Quilmes, Buenos Aires province, Argentina | [USD 65,000 in bitcoins according to the ruling of TOCF No. 2 of La Plata (2020 crypto press reports mentioned 4 BTC, figures ~USD 35,000); 56.5 BTC / ~USD 650,000 initially demanded — On Tuesday, October 13, 2020, Javier Moscuzza, a 46-year-old shopkeeper who owned a supermarket ("Don Santiago", at Allison Bell and Alsina streets) in Quilmes, was intercepted by a gang in the city of Buenos Aires and then held captive for five days in a house in Los Hornos (La Plata). The kidnappers sent proof-of-life and extortion messages via WhatsApp from Colombian and Venezuelan phone numbers, initially demanding the equivalent of USD 650,000; after negotiation, his brother Pablo transferred a ransom in bitcoins (USD 65,000 according to the ruling, 4 BTC according to the press at the time) and the victim was released on October 18 in Quilmes. In November 2024, the Federal Oral Court No. 2 of La Plata sentenced four defendants to terms of 8 to 11 years, while the gang leader Rafael Ángel Carrillo Rodríguez, a former employee of the supermarket, was convicted in Venezuela after fleeing there, and an accomplice remains at large in Brazil; this was the first Argentine kidnapping in which the ransom was paid in cryptocurrency.](https://www.infobae.com/sociedad/policiales/2020/10/20/quilmes-secuestraron-durante-cinco-dias-a-un-comerciante-y-lo-liberan-tras-el-cobro-de-un-rescate-en-bitcoins/) [(source 2)](https://diario5dias.com.ar/noticia/Condenas-secuestro-comerciante-Quilmes-Centro-criptomonedas) |

| December 29 2020 | Mr. Li | Xiamen, Fujian, China | [105,980 USDT (agreed counterpart: CNY 670,000 in cash) — Late 2020, Mr. Li sought to sell USDT and was put in contact by an intermediary with a buyer, Chen, with the price set at CNY 670,000 in cash to be handed over in person in front of a residence in Siming District. Police arrested them on the afternoon of May 11, 2021, before any act was carried out and before the other accomplices recruited online arrived. | May 20 2021 | Chen Chia-hsien | Taichung, Taiwan | [USDT 37,800 (26,000 + 4,800 + 7,000, valued by the court at TWD 1,081,080 at a rate of TWD 28.6/USDT) + 2 Rolex watches + TWD 85,000 in cash (80,000 + 5,000) — After being introduced to the three investors under the LINE alias "Lei Luo" and visiting them in Zhubei on May 18, 2021, the gang lured them on May 20 to an "investment training" dinner at the panoramic Shanjicheng restaurant in Beitun district, Taichung. At 7:08 p.m., on the signal "we're starting to eat" sent in a Telegram group, five masked men lying in wait upstairs burst into the private dining room armed with a machete, baseball bats and an object resembling a firearm; they bound the victims with cable ties, blindfolded them, seized their phones and a Rolex, then had them driven by two Uber drivers to a mountainous area of Sanyi, Miaoli. There, beaten with bats and kicked, and threatened at gunpoint (one was injured on the head, a cut to the forehead), the victims handed over their access codes: 26,000, 4,800 and 7,000 USDT were transferred, a second Rolex and TWD 85,000 were taken, and they were released around 10-11 p.m.; the offense recorded was armed group robbery and kidnapping for ransom (強盜擄人勒贖).](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TCDM%2C110%2C%E9%87%8D%E8%A8%B4%2C1772%2C20220727%2C6) [(source 2)](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TPSM%2C112%2C%E5%8F%B0%E4%B8%8A%2C1309%2C20230517%2C1) |

BRL 1,100,000) — On May 29, 2021, in Balneário Camboriú, two businessmen aged 35 and 41 held captive a 22-year-old young man they had recruited to place about 2 million BRL on sports betting sites and who had diverted part of the funds into cryptocurrency; one of the two had also taken out a loan of six bitcoins (approx. 1.1 million BRL). The victim was held for three days in a penthouse of a luxury condominium rented by one of the kidnappers, guarded by a 37-year-old man and threatened with a firearm, his release being conditional on payment of 1 million BRL; his brother paid 50,000 BRL. Testifying at the federal trial of Remy Ra St Felix (North Carolina, 2024), Seemungal confirmed under oath that he had been "home invaded" and robbed of all his crypto at gunpoint; he said that this robbery, and one suffered by his accomplice "Tidy"/"Neb", led him in summer 2022 to set up the crypto home-invasion network he ran with St Felix.](https://www.bloomberg.com/features/2026-crypto-thieves-kidnappers/) [(source 2)](https://storage.courtlistener.com/recap/gov.uscourts.ncmd.96201/gov.uscourts.ncmd.96201.115.0.pdf) |

Held captive for nearly eleven hours, forced to call his relatives and claim to have cash available to buy USDT (with a video of banknotes supplied by the attackers), he obtained a transfer of USDT 200,000 from his friend Chiu Jo-yu (邱若瑜) to a wallet provided by the abductors, which was resold for TWD 5,500,000 recovered in Sanchong; TWD 100,000 was also transferred from his Taishin bank account through forced facial unlock. Three vehicles cut him off on 1502 Street in Bağcılar; men posing as police officers forced him into a minibus, drove him to a parking lot where they beat him and threatened to kill him to obtain the passwords to his crypto account, converted his cryptocurrency and transferred USD 30,000 to their own accounts, took TL 5,000 in cash from his car, and made him sign a promissory note for USD 200,000, before abandoning him blindfolded in the middle of the street. | October 1 2021 | Unidentified man | Hoorn / Middelie, North Holland, Netherlands | [almost EUR 190,000 in cryptocurrency — On October 1, 2021 at around 7:00 a.m., in a parking lot in Hoorn (North Holland), a man was approached by an individual who pointed a firearm at his head; two accomplices then appeared, took his car keys and forced him into the back seat of his own vehicle, where his wrists were bound with cable ties. During a one-hour drive, under threat of the weapon, he had to hand over his phones as well as the access codes to his phone and his crypto wallet management app; abandoned alone and without a phone in Middelie, he discovered that almost EUR 190,000 in cryptocurrency had been stolen, transferred to the attackers' wallets. The mastermind was a work colleague who knew about his crypto holdings: on April 29, 2024, the Rechtbank Noord-Holland sentenced him to 4 years in prison and his two accomplices to 42 months each, along with nearly EUR 200,000 in joint damages (including EUR 10,000 for moral damages) and confiscation of the proceeds; the victim, long unable to work, had left the Netherlands for his country of birth out of fear.](https://www.rechtspraak.nl/Organisatie-en-contact/Organisatie/Rechtbanken/Rechtbank-Noord-Holland/Nieuws/Paginas/Celstraffen-voor-verdachten-overval-en-roof-cryptovaluta-in-Hoorn.aspx) [(source 2)](https://www.blikopnieuws.nl/nieuws/302197/verdachten-die-slachtoffer-gijzelden-en-beroofden-van-cryptovaluta-moeten-cel-in.html) |

| November 2021 | Five people | Castelo Branco, Portugal | [Unknown (bitcoin transfer not completed) — In November 2021, fourteen months after the first raid, the same foreign criminal group carried out a second violent, "paramilitary-style" action in the Castelo Branco / Idanha-a-Nova area, again aiming to seize cryptocurrency held by foreign nationals residing in Portugal. Five people were held captive, but this time the attackers failed to carry out the bitcoin transfer they had planned. The case was prosecuted under the charges of roubo agravado (aggravated robbery) and five counts of sequestro (unlawful confinement), joined with those from the 2020 raid (Operation "Miragem").](https://www.policiajudiciaria.pt/roubo-bitcoins-operacao-miragem1/) [(source 2)](https://www.policiajudiciaria.pt/extraditado-do-reino-unido-para-portugal-por-roubo-agravado-e-sequestro/) |
| November 2021 | 6 victims | Haifa / Acre / Atlit / Tirat Carmel, Israel | [About ILS 680,000 according to the indictment (up to approx. ILS 1,000,000 across six robberies according to police); an attempted ILS 145,000 theft from undercover officers — Between summer and November 2021, a gang from Acre, Jadeidi and Haifa used Telegram to contact people looking to buy cryptocurrency for cash below market price, arranged meetings with them in northern Israel (the Haifa region, Atlit, Acre), and then robbed them using fake or compressed-air pistols, in broad daylight, in the middle of town and near a school, injuring several victims who had come with suitcases of cash. An undercover operation by the Lahav 433 cyber unit and the "Gidonim" unit (with the Acre and Tirat Carmel police stations) trapped the gang, which attempted to steal 145,000 shekels from undercover officers before being arrested. On November 25, 2021, seven defendants were indicted for aggravated robbery and criminal conspiracy before the Haifa District Court, with total damages estimated at about 680,000 shekels (up to 1 million according to police).](https://www.ice.co.il/law/news/article/836425) [(source 2)](https://www.mako.co.il/nexter-news/Article-9a3e21650815d71027.htm) |
| November 2021 | Unidentified man | Dubai, United Arab Emirates | [243 units of cryptocurrency valued at AED 2,500,000 — In November 2021, five Asian nationals attacked an investor as he was parking his car near his home in the Al Barari neighborhood of Dubai: one threatened him with a weapon and death, a second hit him, and a third placed a black plastic bag over his head before putting him in a vehicle. Tortured during the drive and then at a secret location — the attackers pushed needles under his fingernails and struck him in the face — he eventually made five transfers totaling 243 units of cryptocurrency valued at AED 2.5 million to the kidnappers' wallets, the motive being a financial dispute with one of them. The five defendants, two of whom were fugitives, were sentenced to three years in prison, an AED 2.5 million fine and deportation, a sentence upheld on appeal on January 27, 2022.](https://gulfnews.com/uae/crime/dubai-appeals-court-upholds-sentence-against-gang-that-kidnapped-businessman-and-robbed-dh25-million-in-cryptocurrency-1.85245423) [(source 2)](https://www.khaleejtimes.com/uae/crime/dubai-5-jailed-for-kidnapping-forcing-businessman-to-transfer-dh2-5-million-in-digital-coins) |

| November 8 2021 | Su | Kaohsiung, Taiwan | [TWD 2,600,000 in cash (proceeds from the USDT sale) — Two Kaohsiung residents, Su (38) and Zhao (34), had traveled to Taichung to sell USDT to a couple of buyers for TWD 2.6 million in cash; on the night of November 8, 2021, on the way back, Su was intercepted in his car in Sanmin District by four or five men who took him to a vacant lot in Renwu and beat him with a baseball bat and a stun gun while filming the scene. At dawn on the 9th, the attackers used Su's phone to lure Zhao to Nanzi and beat him until he had a friend bring the 2.6 million. Both victims, having suffered fractures, sought treatment without filing a complaint; Kaohsiung police opened an investigation on their own initiative after the video leaked, with no suspect identified as of the date of the article.](https://news.ltn.com.tw/news/society/breakingnews/3741263) |
| November 10 2021 | Emeka Okoro | Calabar, Nigeria | [NGN 1,700,000 in total (NGN 1,000,000 on October 19, 2021, NGN 700,000 on November 10, 2021) — Emeka Okoro, a cryptocurrency trader in Calabar, was extorted twice in three weeks by police officers from the Akim station: on October 19, 2021, he was stopped in the street in Effio Ette with his brother, had a gun pointed at him and their phones seized, and was ordered to pay 2 million naira or be handed over to the EFCC, ending up paying 1 million; on November 10, armed police officers broke into his apartment, called him a fraudster, demanded 10 million naira and extorted 700,000 naira from him, with the officer holding him at gunpoint being drunk. Subsequently harassed by threatening calls and texts from the gang leader known as "Sir Lion," he fled Calabar with his brother, abandoning his belongings; a spokesperson for the Cross River police promised FIJ full reimbursement.](https://fij.ng/article/crypto-trader-flees-calabar-after-police-extorted-n1-7m-from-him-in-3-weeks/) |
| November 11 2021 | Sun Chen-jung and Pei Wen-hsin | Taoyuan, Taiwan | [TWD 9,990,000 in cash (4,000,000 belonging to Sun Chen-jung, 5,990,000 to Pei Wen-hsin and four other people) — Wang Sheng-hung (王盛弘) gained his victims' trust through a Facebook ad for selling USDT and the Telegram alias "Alan", carrying out three genuine transactions on October 20, November 1 and November 6, 2021 in front of a Tai Ji Men dojo in Taoyuan. On November 11 at 8:15 PM he had Lu Wei-chen (陸韋辰) disguise the license plate of their car near Nanxing Park in Daxi, then at 9:46 PM the two buyers got into the vehicle; once the money was counted, the robbers pointed a realistic-looking air pistol and a second weapon at them, with Wang showing the magazine containing a real military round to claim the weapons were real, before making off with TWD 9,990,000 and ejecting the victims at two separate locations. Sun Chen-jung alerted a patrol at 10:10 PM, but the attackers forced their way past the checkpoint and fled to a garage in Changhua; they were sentenced to 10 years and 8 years 2 months in prison for armed robbery, sentences upheld on appeal.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TYDM%2C111%2C%E8%A8%B4%2C273%2C20220630%2C3) [(source 2)](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TPHM%2C111%2C%E4%B8%8A%E8%A8%B4%2C2855%2C20230307%2C3) |

| November 12 2021 | Mr. Murillo and his father | La Fortuna de Bagaces, Guanacaste, Costa Rica | [approximately CRC 200,000,000 in cryptocurrency (~USD 310,000) — On November 12, 2021, a man named Varela set up a fake bitcoin purchase: through a broker (Rodriguez), he lured cryptocurrency holders to a ranch at a tourist center in La Fortuna de Bagaces (Guanacaste) — a man named Murillo and his father, then a certain Zhang, who held larger sums. Five armed accomplices emerged from the kitchen, tied the victims' hands and feet, taped their mouths, beat them and threatened to kill them; Rodriguez fled under gunfire without being hit, Murillo was wounded in the arm, buttock and leg, and Zhang was forced to transfer approximately 200 million colones. Murillo and Zhang, doused with flammable liquid and locked in a car trunk, managed to escape en route, while a third victim, Jimenez, was taken near a national park and burned alive in the vehicle to destroy evidence; Varela, who had gone to the OIJ in Liberia posing as a victim, was sentenced to 50 years in prison.](https://crhoy.com/nacionales/secuestros-calcinamiento-y-asesinato-todo-lo-que-hizo-hombre-para-robar-mas-de-%E2%82%A1200-millones-en-criptomonedas/) |
| November 19 2021 | 34-year-old man | Orlando, Florida, United States | [approximately USD 650 stolen (USD 200 in cash + USD 450 via an app); USD 30,000 contract extorted — On November 19, 2021, in a house rented via Airbnb on Skyview Drive in Orlando, Amony Robillard (30), a Brevard County Sheriff's corrections officer, emerged from behind a curtain with two semi-automatic pistols as the victim, a 34-year-old man, was coming out of the shower, and held him at gunpoint naked for about three hours. He forced him to sign a USD 30,000 contract — the amount he claimed to have lost on a crypto deal recommended by the victim — by bringing in a notary and the victim's wife, and threatened to release compromising videos to his family and his church; USD 200 in cash and USD 450 via an app were stolen. Robillard was charged with armed robbery, armed extortion and armed unlawful confinement, and his accomplice Jerise Richer (19) with armed robbery and conspiracy to commit extortion.](https://www.clickorlando.com/news/local/2022/01/07/brevard-sheriffs-deputy-held-naked-man-at-gunpoint-for-hours-during-armed-robbery-report-shows/) |

| December 2021 | Michael Monteverde & Antoine Luce | Sainte-Anne-des-Lacs, Laurentides, Canada | [7.70 ETH (~USD 30,000) + luxury watches and jewelry — In December 2021, French crypto influencer Michael Monteverde ('mickalamasse') and his associate Antoine Luce, who had come to Quebec to meet a business contact, were lured to a chalet in Sainte-Anne-des-Lacs where, in the middle of the night, three masked men armed with rifles broke in, beat Monteverde with a speaker and a bottle, broke down the bathroom door where he had taken refuge, tied him up then plunged his face into a jug of water to force him to open his eyes and unlock his phone by facial recognition. | February 28 2022 | Liliana Segovia Navarro | Barranquilla, Colombia | [more than USD 10,000 in cryptocurrency + a pickup truck — Jairo Medina Vega, having learned that Liliana Segovia Navarro (36) owned a new pickup truck and more than 10,000 dollars in cryptocurrency, contacted her via social media and arranged a meeting under the pretext of a business deal on February 28, 2022; he struck her and then asphyxiated her in the vehicle, concealed the body in a plastic drum at his apartment in the Betania neighborhood before dumping it in an uninhabited area near Tubara (Atlantico), then robbed her by electronically transferring her crypto assets. | March 10 2022 | Sylwester Suszek | Czeladz, Silesia, Poland | [ransom in bitcoin, amount undisclosed (about 4,500 BTC potentially under his exclusive control) — Sylwester Suszek, founder of the Polish exchange platform BitBay (later Zonda, then Zondacrypto), disappeared on March 10, 2022, after going to a meeting at a fuel depot in Czeladz with an associate linked to organized crime; his phone signal cut out shortly after 3:08 p.m., and his white Porsche Taycan was found abandoned. His family subsequently received messages claiming he had been kidnapped and demanding a ransom in bitcoin, with his sister receiving death threats among other things. | March 16 2022 | 32-year-old woman | San Francisco, California, United States | [USD 3,000,000 in cryptocurrency — On March 16, 2022, a man wearing a ski mask and goggles broke into the home of a 32-year-old woman in Bernal Heights (San Francisco), threatened her with a knife and bound her with tape, then communicated his orders by typing them on an iPad to avoid being identified by voice. | March 21 2022 | Yu | Taichung, Taiwan | [140,000 USDT demanded (about TWD 4,000,000); TWD 5,000,000 in promissory notes (10 x TWD 500,000) and more than TWD 2,000,000 in extorted loans; crypto wallet and online bank accounts emptied — On the evening of March 21, 2022, in Taichung, a woman named Zhuo lured her ex-boyfriend Yu into a motel room, then, under the pretext of a food delivery, let in about ten men sent by Xu, who suspected Yu of having misappropriated his 140,000 USDT (about TWD 4 million). Yu was stripped, blindfolded and tied up, beaten with bars, and plunged into a bathtub of ice-cold water and then boiling water until he handed over his codes: his crypto wallet and online bank accounts were emptied, and he was forced to sign ten TWD 500,000 promissory notes and take out more than TWD 2 million in loans before being released in early April. | April 1 2022 | Ifeoluwa Lawal | Ede, Osun State, Nigeria | [USD 504 in crypto transferred from a Binance account — On Friday, April 1, 2022, officers of the Osun State Police Command stopped the vehicle of Ifeoluwa Lawal, a Nigerian student based in Cyprus who was returning to Abeokuta with his family, near Sekona Grammar School in Ede; one of them took his phone and found a screenshot of his Binance app on it, which led to him being called a "Yahoo boy" and threatened with being handed over to the EFCC. The officers successively demanded NGN 50,000, then USD 2,000, then USD 500, moving the family from place to place for hours and striking his brother, injuring his knee with a van door; he eventually scanned a QR code for a wallet address provided by the officers and transferred USD 504 from his Binance account. | May 5 2022 | Costin Miu | Mamaia, Constanța, Romania | [no crypto transfer obtained; alleged debt of EUR 3,000,000, ransom promised of EUR 200,000 to 300,000 — On May 5, 2022, at around 10:30 a.m., Costin Miu, a businessman who had grown wealthy in cryptocurrency, was attacked in the parking lot of the Coral residential building in Mamaia as he was getting into his luxury car: Alexandru Ioan Dorobanțu, a drug trafficker from Constanța, struck him in the head with an elbow before three Nigerian nationals sent from London overpowered him, bound his wrists with tape, and put a hood over his head. Held captive for several hours at the former Năvodari camp, bound, hooded and stabbed (with a syringe or knife, according to differing press accounts) in the abdomen, he was ordered to hand over the passwords to his crypto wallets, with the London-based mastermind claiming to be recovering a debt of EUR 3 million arising from computer fraud. Alerted by neighbors via the 112 emergency number, police foiled the operation: Miu, released after promising EUR 200,000 to 300,000, told the Mamaia police station everything; Dorobanțu was arrested on the spot and sentenced to 7 years and 4 months in prison, while the three Nigerians remain at large.](https://www.digi24.ro/stiri/un-milionar-in-criptomonede-din-mamaia-a-fost-rapit-de-trei-nigerieni-din-parcarea-blocului-scenariul-de-film-descris-de-procurori-2352587) [(source 2)](https://www.gandul.ro/actualitate/cum-a-fost-rapit-un-milionar-in-criptomonede-din-mamaia-victima-intepata-cu-o-seringa-cu-ac-in-abdomen-19980420) |

| January 18 2024 | Roberto Felipe Mejía Caicedo | Envigado, Antioquia, Colombia | [USD 184,000 actually transferred in cryptocurrency (approx. COP 720-730 million); COP 3,000 million demanded in total (about USD 750,000) — On January 18, 2024, Roberto Felipe Mejía Caicedo, 43, an influencer and businessman from Cali (460,000 followers), was lured to the shopping center in the La Frontera area, on the border between Medellín and Envigado, under the pretext of closing a million-peso cryptocurrency deal, then abducted and taken to a country house (casa finca) in La Loma del Chocho, Envigado, where he was held captive for about 36 hours, gagged, bound, mistreated and threatened with death. His kidnappers, described as members of the criminal group "La Oficina," demanded 3,000 million pesos; the victim was forced to transfer 184,000 dollars in cryptocurrency before managing to alert his bodyguard by a WhatsApp message. The CTI of the Fiscalía and the army's Gaula unit located the house, freed the victim unharmed and arrested five kidnappers on the spot, seizing five firearms, two vehicles and more than 6 million pesos.](https://www.infobae.com/colombia/2024/01/20/cti-libero-a-comerciante-secuestrado-en-medellin-durante-una-negociacion-de-criptomonedas/) [(source 2)](https://www.eltiempo.com/colombia/medellin/envigado-la-millonada-en-criptomonedas-que-influencer-secuestrado-pago-a-captores-847631) |

Impersonating the victim on his phone, the attackers had one of Liu's contacts transfer 84 and then 21,000 USDT (≈TWD 700,000) at 10:12 p.m. and 10:15 p.m., took TWD 10,000 in cash from him, and then threw him out of the vehicle in Shulin (New Taipei) at around 10:35 p.m. Arrested on June 24 in a rented apartment in Taishan (a machete and cable ties were seized in the vehicle), Lin and intermediary Chang Chih-chia were sentenced to 7 years 6 months and 7 years 8 months respectively for aggravated robbery with a weapon, sentences upheld on appeal on December 24, 2025.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=SLDM%2C113%2C%E5%8E%9F%E8%A8%B4%2C32%2C20241029%2C2) [(source 2)](https://www.chinatimes.com/realtimenews/20241030003512-260402) |

The same gang attacked a cryptocurrency trainer and his wife a week later (WA279).](https://www.ettoday.net/news/20260316/3133131.htm) |
| February 15 2025 | A cryptocurrency industry trainer and his wife | Ximending, Taipei, Taiwan | [TWD 13,000,000 (cash + cryptocurrencies) + a Maserati — In mid-February 2025, a man working in cryptocurrency training and his wife were lured to Ximending (Taipei) under the pretext of a video shoot whose script had been arranged the day before; on arrival, the attackers changed the meeting location and nine men dressed in black surrounded them, handcuffed them, beat them and threatened them with a knife and a firearm to force them to transfer all their cash and cryptoassets, amounting to TWD 13 million, and to take their Maserati. Held captive for a day and a half, photographed naked under duress, the victim had his head split open against a steel bar (two stitches); more than five months after the complaint was filed, no suspect had been arrested, with the couple stating that the gang had already robbed one of their friends a week earlier.](https://news.tvbs.com.tw/local/2931456) [(source 2)](https://www.ettoday.net/news/20260316/3133131.htm) |
| February 19 2025 | Armando Taveres | Sao Pedro do Sul / Viseu, Portugal | ["several million dollars" in dividends demanded; "thousands of bitcoins" believed to be on the device (actual amount never established) — In February 2025, a group of four foreign men (aged 27 to 46, linked according to the press to a London-based gang acting for an English financial company), assisted by a Portuguese businesswoman living in Porto, targeted a 42-year-old Luso-Brazilian financial intermediary at his home in Sao Pedro do Sul, allegedly the holder of codes allowing the release of several million dollars in dividends. Under coercion and violence, they held him and forced him, on 20 February, to go and retrieve a digital device from a bank safe deposit box in Viseu, supposedly containing thousands of bitcoins; the victim claimed a technical problem requiring a second device, returned to the bank and raised the alarm, which triggered the intervention of the PSP and then the Judicial Police. Four men were arrested and placed in pretrial detention, followed the next day by a Portuguese businesswoman; all five were subsequently convicted (judgment reported in August 2026, sentence amounts not verified).](https://www.policiajudiciaria.pt/criminalidade-violenta-pj-detem-cinco-pessoas-por-crimes-de-rapto-e-de-roubo/) [(source 2)](https://executivedigest.sapo.pt/noticias/gangue-de-londres-rapta-empresario-em-viseu-para-cobrar-divida-milionaria/) |

| March 2025 | Naiping Hou | Rancho Cucamonga, California, United States | [more than USD 1,000,000 siphoned off (cash, gold bars and cryptocurrency) — Naiping (Nai Ping) Hou, 74, father of Wen Hou, chief investment officer of the crypto fund Coincident Capital, was last seen in mid-March 2025 at his home in Rancho Cucamonga while his wife was in China; he was not reported missing until 4 May 2025, during a wellness check that found the house completely emptied of furniture and belongings. More than a million dollars were siphoned from his accounts through withdrawals, unauthorized sales of his vehicles, and purchases of gold and cryptocurrency, and text messages with "robotic" phrasing were sent to his family from his phone. Searches uncovered in Loverval (Gerpinnes, Hainaut) a room entirely lined with plastic sheeting serving as a torture room, where cable ties, saws, pliers, hammers, box cutters, nails and adhesive tape were seized; the group planned to kidnap and torture two teenagers aged 13 and 17, relatives of an investor who had grown wealthy in cryptocurrency, in order to obtain a ransom. In March 2026, the Brussels court convicted seven defendants, two to 14 years and five to 12 years in prison; the suspected mastermind, Badiss Mohamed Amide Bajjou, 24, arrested in Tangier and linked to several crypto kidnappings in France, is the subject of an extradition request.](https://www.vrt.be/vrtnws/en/2026/03/19/stiff-sentences-after-police-uncover-belgian-torture-chamber/) [(source 2)](https://www.crypto-insiders.nl/nieuws/overheid-en-regulatie/celstraffen-voor-bende-die-belgische-kinderen-wilde-martelen-voor-crypto/) |

Alerted by her early in the morning, police located the vehicle, arrested the kidnappers at 3:16 p.m. and 3:26 p.m. in a parking lot in Shilin, and freed the victim in under eight hours, seizing handcuffs, 3 baseball bats, 7 machetes and 2 stun batons.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=SLDM%2c114%2c%e9%87%8d%e8%a8%b4%2c2%2c20251216%2c2) [(source 2)](https://www.chinatimes.com/realtimenews/20251217001653-260402) |

EUR 500,000) and 20 gold coins (EUR 60,000) — approximately EUR 3,000,000 in total loot — At around 3 a.m. on Monday, August 11, 2025, five masked men armed with pistols, equipped with a powerful signal jammer and guided by phone by a sixth accomplice, broke into the villa of crypto entrepreneur Álvaro Romillo, alias 'CryptoSpain', in a private gated community in San Sebastián de los Reyes, shortly after his return from Ibiza. Romillo and his partner were gagged, bound hand and foot with tape and cable ties, and held for about four hours, with pistols pointed at their heads after a round was chambered, under death threats; the attackers demanded EUR 40 million in cryptocurrency and obtained, from the victim's phone, a forced transfer of EUR 1,300,000, in addition to about EUR 200,000 to 210,000 in cash, jewelry, more than 30 watches (approx. EUR 500,000) and 20 gold coins (EUR 60,000), for a total of about EUR 3 million. On May 28, 2026, the Policía Nacional announced the dismantling of the network: six Venezuelans aged 25 to 35 linked to Tren de Aragua were arrested after six searches in Madrid, Murcia and Camarma de Esteruelas, three of them remanded in pretrial detention, for this home invasion and two other similar incidents in tourist rental apartments, in one of which a resisting victim was injured in the face by a gunshot.](https://www.interior.gob.es/opencms/eu/detalle/articulo/La-Policia-Nacional-desarticula-un-entramado-criminal-de-El-tren-de-Aragua-dedicado-a-la-comision-de-robos-con-violencia/) [(source 2)](https://www.democrata.es/tribunales/intrusos-residencia-alvaro-romillo-buscaban-apropiarse-40-millones-criptomonedas-amenazaron-asesinato/) |

Once the victims were unable to react, the trio accessed their accounts and withdrew GBP 2,100, including GBP 300 converted into cryptocurrencies and GBP 1,800 in four transactions, while an attempted transfer of GBP 16,000 failed due to facial recognition; footage shows the three women getting into a taxi shortly afterward, with the victims visibly intoxicated. The Rio public prosecutor's office (MPRJ) indicted them in August 2025 for theft with improper violence, theft aggravated by electronic fraud, and criminal association, and requested their pretrial detention.](https://www.cnnbrasil.com.br/nacional/sudeste/rj/mprj-denuncia-trio-por-dopar-e-roubar-turistas-ingleses-no-rio/) |

| August 2025 | A man | Utrecht, Netherlands | [more than EUR 40,000 in cryptocurrencies (plus cash, USB keys, car keys, laptop, phone, wallet) — On August 25, 2025, four men wearing jackets marked "Justitie" (justice) showed up at the door of a resident of the Zeeheldenbuurt neighborhood in Utrecht and held him captive for several hours in his own home: a towel hood over his head, a t-shirt stuffed in his mouth, ankles and wrists bound, then pushed, still bound, under the shower, with a firearm constantly pointed at him, in order to obtain his login credentials and passwords and empty his crypto account. The haul exceeded EUR 40,000 in cryptocurrencies, plus cash, USB keys, car keys, a laptop, a phone and a wallet; the victim, injured, had to be hospitalized. Several suspects were arrested; a 23-year-old from Amsterdam appeared at a pro forma hearing at the Utrecht court on February 16, 2026, acknowledging a logistical role ("I was supposed to take care of the cars"), while another suspect was released for lack of evidence.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260217212623/https://www.ad.nl/utrecht/overvallers-roofden-40-000-euro-aan-cryptovaluta-utrechts-slachtoffer-gekneveld-en-onder-de-douche-gezet~a0dcc844/) [(source 2)](https://www.rtvutrecht.nl/nieuws/3933697/bewoner-urenlang-vastgehouden-in-huis-door-overvallers-met-wapen) |

| September 5 2025 | Nguyen Van D | Quang Hung commune, Hung Yen province, Vietnam | [VND 3,000,000,000 in cash (3 billion dong) — On the morning of September 5, 2025, in Quang Hung commune (Hung Yen province), Mr. Nguyen Van D., 33, who had come from Da Nang with 3 billion dong in cash (about USD 114,000) to buy USDT, was lured into a fake cash-for-crypto exchange meeting: four men staged the transaction, snatched the bag of cash and fled, then scattered across several provinces. | September 6 2025 | USDT buyer anonymized as "A01" in the ruling | Chiayi, Taiwan | [TWD 300,000 (price agreed for 10,000 USDT) — A group organized on Telegram (using the aliases "李純青" and "徐乃麟") lured a buyer by offering 10,000 USDT for TWD 300,000, arranging a meeting on September 5, 2025 at 11:30 p.m. at the 7-Eleven Jiatai in Chiayi. The case (FIR no. 18 of 09/17/2025, Cyber Crime police station, Khanna, Ludhiana district) led to several arrests, including Karandeep Singh (09/17/2025) and Khushdev Singh @ Munny (10/10/2025), who were released on bail on March 11, 2026, with the court noting the existence of a larger criminal syndicate.](https://indiankanoon.org/doc/146703775/) [(source 2)](https://indiankanoon.org/doc/179079338/) |

| September 19 2025 | Unidentified | Arnhem, Netherlands | [Undisclosed cryptocurrency amount; one automatic CZ 858 firearm stolen — On the morning of September 19, 2025, a young man disguised as a parcel courier (wearing a DHL uniform, according to De Gelderlander) rang the doorbell of a house on Bakenbergseweg in Arnhem; as soon as the door opened, he rushed inside followed by three other men. | October 9 2025 | Sneh Parmar | Indore, Madhya Pradesh, India | [5,773 USDT (about INR 5.12 lakh) — On October 9, 2025 between 6 and 7 p.m., three men posing as Crime Branch officers broke into Sneh Parmar's home in Dwarkapuri (Gandhi Chowk), accused him of fraud, beat him and his father, and drove them to the Lasudia area. | October 14 2025 | USDT buyer anonymized as "A02" in the ruling | Taichung, Dali District, Taiwan | [TWD 1,100,000 in cash (amount at stake; ultimately NOT taken — attempt) — Directed via Telegram by accounts using the pseudonyms "十大通緝犯" (the ten most-wanted criminals) and "普利司通"/"智幣Smartcoin", four men lured the buyer "A02" in front of the Qijiangjun Temple in Dali (Taichung) on October 14, 2025 at 3:55 PM for an exchange of TWD 1,100,000 for USDT, after fitting fake license plates to their vehicle 30 minutes earlier. Once the victim was seated in the back seat with the bag of cash and the money counted, "A04" sprayed him with pepper spray while the driver Li Jiajin drove toward the point where two accomplices were waiting to restrain him; but A02 managed to grab back the TWD 1.1 million and jump from the moving vehicle, causing the robbery to fail. An accomplice who got into the back of the victim's car counted the money, then two others opened the doors and pepper-sprayed the faces of the victim and the person with him; when the victim refused to let go of the bag, the first accomplice stabbed him in the leg, and the attackers fled without the money. | October 18 2025 | Ms. Ding | Taipei, Nangang District, Taiwan | [TWD 1,920,000 — On October 18, 2025 in the afternoon, Ms. Ding, 48, parked on Nangang Road (Section 1) in Taipei with TWD 1,920,000 in cash to buy USDT from a "broker" met online through a friend. A man named Tang, 31, got into the back seat, never transferred the tokens, then forcibly grabbed the cash and fled on foot, with the victim chasing him and shouting "thief". Police intercepted him about 20 minutes later on Kangning Road (Section 3) in Neihu, in his rental car: the money was recovered, Tang turned out to be a "mule" (車手) for a fraud network, and he was referred for prosecution for fraud and robbery with violence (搶奪) and then placed in pretrial detention.](https://news.ltn.com.tw/news/society/breakingnews/5217822) |
| October 29 2025 | Mr. Chen | Taipei, Taiwan | [TWD 3,000,000 in real banknotes (of which TWD 781,600 recovered); initial fraud of TWD 280,000 — Mr. Chen, already defrauded of TWD 280,000 in July 2025 by a "broker" named Lin whose cryptocurrency had disappeared from his cold wallet, set up a sting operation with the Zhongshan police station in late October: a new meeting on Minquan East Road (Section 3), near a convenience store, for a staged purchase of TWD 3 million, with the victim refusing the counterfeit bills offered by police. On October 29, 2025 around 4 PM, the mule Pu grabbed the bag; when the police lying in wait intervened, two minor accomplices attacked them with a baseball bat and a bladed weapon, injuring two officers, while another accomplice picked up the bag and Lin sped off.
